# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccountAssignment

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAssignment.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| instanceId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":64} |
| estimatedLoad | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "estimatedLoad": 1,
  "instanceId": "example",
  "role": "example"
}
```

## AccountAvailability

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAvailability.json), input validation.

Type: ["available","near_limit","exhausted","logged_out","unknown"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"available"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

Type: ["CODEX_HOME","CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR","XDG_DATA_HOME","XDG_CONFIG_HOME","XDG_STATE_HOME","XDG_CACHE_HOME","CURSOR_DATA_DIR"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

```json
"CURSOR_DATA_DIR"
```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| cursorSdkAuth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": "logged_out",
  "blockers": {
    "limitError": {
      "resetsAt": 0,
      "usedPercent": 4
    }
  },
  "observedAt": 9,
  "usage": {
    "costUsd": 5,
    "outputTokens": 1
  },
  "windows": {}
}
```

```json
      "source": "sdk-store",

    "usage": {},
    "windows": {}
```

```json
}
```

## Agent

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| origin | yes | [AgentOrigin.json](schema/AgentOrigin.json) |  |
| lineage | no | [ThreadLineage.json](schema/ThreadLineage.json) |  |
| native | yes | [NativeRef.json](schema/NativeRef.json) |  |
| fidelity | yes | [AgentFidelity.json](schema/AgentFidelity.json) |  |
| spawnedBy | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ItemId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| name | no | string |  |
| role | no | string |  |
| model | no | string |  |
| cwd | yes | string |  |
| status | yes | [AgentStatus.json](schema/AgentStatus.json) |  |

| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

```json
  "background": true,

  "cwd": "example",
  "endedAt": 7,
  "fidelity": "full",
  "id": "example",
  "lineage": {
    "lossy": true,
    "mode": "native",
    "parentAgentId": "example",
    "parentThreadId": "example",
    "point": {
      "itemId": "example",
      "type": "item"
    }
  },
  "name": "example",
  "native": {
    "forkedFromNativeId": "example",
    "installationId": "example",
    "path": "example",
    "provider": "opencode"
  },
  "origin": "provider_subagent",
  "parentId": null,
  "status": {
    "state": "interrupted"
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

Type: ["auto","embedded","headless"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"auto"
```

## BrowserBackendRegister

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserBackendRegister.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"browser.backend.register"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| credential | yes | string | {"pattern":"^[a-f0-9]{64}$"} |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| capabilities | yes | [BrowserBackendCapabilities.json](schema/BrowserBackendCapabilities.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "capabilities": {
    "cdp": true,
    "controllerLease": true,
    "downloads": true,
    "permissions": true,
    "targets": true
  },
  "credential": "f9e64cc4d7726f2f7a29ebe8a2bde7b6789851ec9be187cabf23050e730a4d32",
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "browser.backend.register",
  "version": 1
}
```

## BrowserBackendRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserBackendRequest.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| backendId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| sessionId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| type | yes | `"browser.backend.request"` |  |
| id | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| operation | yes | [BrowserBackendOperation.json](schema/BrowserBackendOperation.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "backendId": "example",
  "id": "example",
  "operation": {
    "kind": "open",
    "lease": {
      "controller": "none",
      "generation": 9
    },
    "options": {
      "threadId": "example",
      "workspaceId": "example"
    },
    "viewport": {
      "height": 2,
      "width": 8
    }
  },
  "sessionId": "example",
  "type": "browser.backend.request"
}
```

## BrowserBackendResponse

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserBackendResponse.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| backendId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| sessionId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |

```json
}
```

## BrowserCommand

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserCommand.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| timeout | no | integer | {"default":10000,"minimum":1,"maximum":30000} |

Example:

```json

  "action": "navigate",
  "url": "example"
}
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| action | yes | `"click"` |  |

```json
  "ref": "example"
}
```

```json
}
```

```json
  "action": "scroll",

  "y": 2
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

```json
}
```

## BrowserInput

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserInput.json), input validation.

### mouse

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"mouse"` |  |
| event | yes | ["mouseMoved","mousePressed","mouseReleased"] |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| button | no | ["none","left","middle","right"] | {"default":"left"} |
| clickCount | no | integer | {"default":1,"minimum":0,"maximum":3} |

Example:

```json
{
  "event": "mousePressed",
  "kind": "mouse",
  "x": 7,
  "y": 9
}
```

### key

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"key"` |  |
| event | yes | ["keyDown","keyUp","char"] |  |
| key | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| code | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| text | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| modifiers | no | integer | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":15} |

Example:

```json
{
  "event": "keyUp",
  "key": "example",
  "kind": "key"
}
```

### scroll

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"scroll"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| deltaX | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| deltaY | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |

Example:

```json
{
  "deltaX": 8,
  "deltaY": 3,
  "kind": "scroll",
  "x": 3,
  "y": 8
}
```

### touch

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"touch"` |  |
| event | yes | ["touchStart","touchMove","touchEnd","touchCancel"] |  |
| points | yes | array | {"maxItems":10,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"x":{"type":"number","minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000},"y":{"type":"number","minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000},"id":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9}},"required":["x","y","id"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "event": "touchCancel",
  "kind": "touch",
  "points": []
}
```

## Capabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/Capabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| approvals | no | ["interactive","sandbox-only","none"] |  |
| steeringMode | no | ["native","interrupt-restart","queue"] |  |
| forkMode | no | ["native","context-handoff","none"] |  |
| childControls | no | ["native","read-only"] |  |
| childFidelity | no | ["full","summary","placeholder"] |  |
| steer | yes | boolean |  |
| launchOptions | no | array | {"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["effort","serviceTier"]}} |
| interruptCascades | yes | boolean |  |
| resume | yes | boolean |  |
| fork | yes | boolean |  |
| forkSubagents | no | boolean |  |
| sessionOptions | no | boolean |  |
| forkPoints | no | array | {"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["turn","item","end"]}} |
| subagentTranscripts | yes | boolean |  |
| backgroundTaskControl | yes | boolean |  |
| backgroundVisibility | yes | ["full","partial","none"] |  |
| planMode | yes | boolean |  |
| tokenUsage | yes | boolean |  |
| imageInput | yes | boolean |  |
| rewindFiles | yes | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "backgroundTaskControl": false,
  "backgroundVisibility": "none",
  "childControls": "native",
  "fork": false,
  "forkPoints": [],
  "imageInput": false,
  "interruptCascades": true,
  "launchOptions": [],
  "planMode": false,
  "resume": true,
  "rewindFiles": true,
  "steer": false,
  "subagentTranscripts": false,
  "tokenUsage": false
}
```

## CommandPlan

[JSON Schema](schema/CommandPlan.json), input validation.

### native

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"native"` |  |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| text | yes | string | {"maxLength":65536} |
| metadata | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string"},"additionalProperties":{}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "native",
  "metadata": {},
  "provider": "cursor",
  "text": "example"
}
```

### prompt

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"prompt"` |  |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| text | yes | string | {"maxLength":65536} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "prompt",
  "provider": "antigravity",
  "text": "example"
}
```

### ace

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"ace"` |  |
| action | yes | [PaletteName.json](schema/PaletteName.json) |  |
| arguments | yes | [PromptArguments.json](schema/PromptArguments.json) |  |
| positional | yes | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":16384}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": "IAtbY",
  "arguments": {},
  "kind": "ace",
  "positional": []
}
```

## CommandResolution

[JSON Schema](schema/CommandResolution.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ok | yes | `true` |  |
| plan | yes | [CommandPlan.json](schema/CommandPlan.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "ok": true,
  "plan": {
    "kind": "native",
    "metadata": {},
    "provider": "acp",
    "text": "example"
  }
}
```

### Variant 2

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ok | yes | `false` |  |
| error | yes | ["not_found","unsupported_provider","unsupported_command","missing_argument","invalid_argument","limit_exceeded"] |  |
| argument | no | string |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "error": "missing_argument",
  "ok": false
}
```

## CommandsList

[JSON Schema](schema/CommandsList.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| type | yes | `"commands.list"` |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":1,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "commands.list"
}
```

## CommandsListResult

[JSON Schema](schema/CommandsListResult.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"commands.list.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string |  |
| commands | yes | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/PaletteCommand.json"}} |
| diagnostics | yes | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/CommandDiagnostic.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "commands": [],
  "diagnostics": [],
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "commands.list.result"
}
```

## CommandsResolve

[JSON Schema](schema/CommandsResolve.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| type | yes | `"commands.resolve"` |  |
| commandId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| arguments | no | [PromptArguments.json](schema/PromptArguments.json) | {"default":{}} |
| positional | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":64,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":16384}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "commandId": "example",
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "commands.resolve"
}
```

## CommandsResolveResult

[JSON Schema](schema/CommandsResolveResult.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"commands.resolve.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string |  |
| result | yes | [CommandResolution.json](schema/CommandResolution.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "requestId": "example",
  "result": {
    "ok": true,
    "plan": {
      "kind": "prompt",
      "provider": "acp",
      "text": "example"
    }
  },
  "type": "commands.resolve.result"
}
```

## ContextDiagnostic

[JSON Schema](schema/ContextDiagnostic.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| code | yes | [ContextErrorCode.json](schema/ContextErrorCode.json) |  |

## ContextErrorCode

[JSON Schema](schema/ContextErrorCode.json), input validation.

Type: ["invalid_request","forbidden","not_found","outside_workspace","ignored","binary","truncated","quota","busy","offset","hash_mismatch","invalid_image","unsupported"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"binary"
```

## ContextMeter

[JSON Schema](schema/ContextMeter.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| epoch | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| usedTokens | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| windowTokens | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| model | no | string |  |
| source | yes | ["provider","catalog","unknown"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "epoch": 6,
  "source": "provider",
  "usedTokens": null,
  "windowTokens": null
}
```

## ContextMeterUpdated

[JSON Schema](schema/ContextMeterUpdated.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"context_meter.updated"` |  |
| meter | yes | [ContextMeter.json](schema/ContextMeter.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "meter": {
    "agentId": "example",
    "epoch": 4,
    "model": "example",
    "source": "catalog",
    "usedTokens": null,
    "windowTokens": 6
  },
  "type": "context_meter.updated"
}
```

## ContextOperation

[JSON Schema](schema/ContextOperation.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"draft.create"` |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "draft.create",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

### Variant 2

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"draft.release"` |  |
| draftId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "draftId": "Ue0",
  "op": "draft.release"
}
```

### Variant 3

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"draft.upload.begin"` |  |
| draftId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"} |
| sha256 | yes | [BlobHash.json](schema/BlobHash.json) |  |
| bytes | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| name | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":255} |

Example:

```json
{
  "bytes": 10,
  "draftId": "_-ptHXnd95A",
  "name": "example",
  "op": "draft.upload.begin",
  "sha256": "c75b9e9d49029a9627e8a36e79b6669384893d74d4065ca3404fba4aa85ae402"
}
```

### Variant 4

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"draft.mention.complete"` |  |
| draftId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"} |
| query | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":20,"minimum":1,"maximum":50} |

Example:

```json
{
  "draftId": "OJJNNgcKO",
  "limit": 8,
  "op": "draft.mention.complete",
  "query": "example"
}
```

### Variant 5

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"upload.begin"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| sha256 | yes | [BlobHash.json](schema/BlobHash.json) |  |
| bytes | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| name | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":255} |

Example:

```json
{
  "bytes": 8,
  "name": "example",
  "op": "upload.begin",
  "sha256": "62d8995ecd564ccca3a65e1aca1486aaee661fa6c5d4ebe8ea80101e9a128553",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

### Variant 6

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"upload.status"` |  |
| uploadId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "upload.status",
  "uploadId": "gQ94UzzCXvk"
}
```

### Variant 7

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"upload.chunk"` |  |
| uploadId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"} |
| offset | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| data | yes | string | {"minLength":4,"maxLength":87384,"pattern":"^(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{4})*(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{2}==\|[A-Za-z0-9+/]{3}=)?$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "data": "tk+QU89oICtxafoBwOif",
  "offset": 7,
  "op": "upload.chunk",
  "uploadId": "DK"
}
```

### Variant 8

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"upload.commit"` |  |
| uploadId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "upload.commit",
  "uploadId": "WXYuJBbr"
}
```

### Variant 9

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"upload.cancel"` |  |
| uploadId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "upload.cancel",
  "uploadId": "k6"
}
```

### Variant 10

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"attachment.list"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "attachment.list",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

### Variant 11

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"attachment.release"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| sha256 | yes | [BlobHash.json](schema/BlobHash.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "attachment.release",
  "sha256": "54c72d8a3c0c9854dcbcceebe38e8b6dfba9932fdb95748272274a5d02d38217",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

### Variant 12

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"mention.resolve"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| mentions | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Mention.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "mentions": [
    {
      "lines": {
        "end": 10,
        "start": 7
      },
      "path": "example"
    }
  ],
  "op": "mention.resolve",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

### Variant 13

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"mention.complete"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| query | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":20,"minimum":1,"maximum":50} |

Example:

```json
{
  "limit": 8,
  "op": "mention.complete",
  "query": "example",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

## ContextRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ContextRequest.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"context.request"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"} |
| operation | yes | [ContextOperation.json](schema/ContextOperation.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "operation": {
    "draftId": "_jJ",
    "limit": 2,
    "op": "draft.mention.complete",
    "query": "example"
  },
  "requestId": "i8jBWYSs",
  "type": "context.request"
}
```

## ContextResult

[JSON Schema](schema/ContextResult.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"context.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"} |
| result | yes | union | {"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"draft"},"draftId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"}},"required":["kind","draftId"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"error"},"code":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ContextErrorCode.json"},"message":{"type":"string"}},"required":["kind","code","message"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"upload"},"uploadId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"},"offset":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"bytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["kind","uploadId","offset","bytes"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"attachment"},"attachment":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Attachment.json"}},"required":["kind","attachment"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"attachments"},"attachments":{"maxItems":256,"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Attachment.json"}}},"required":["kind","attachments"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"mentions"},"entries":{"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ResolvedMention.json"}},"diagnostics":{"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ContextDiagnostic.json"}}},"required":["kind","entries","diagnostics"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"completion"},"paths":{"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}},"required":["kind","paths"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"ok"}},"required":["kind"]}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "requestId": "XksT",
  "result": {
    "draftId": "aBHbtGEhO0",
    "kind": "draft"
  },
  "type": "context.result"
}
```

## CursorAuthEvent

[JSON Schema](schema/CursorAuthEvent.json), input validation.

### cursor.auth.login

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"cursor.auth.login"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| loginId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| instanceId | yes | [AccountId.json](schema/AccountId.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["starting","browser","complete","failed","cancelled"] |  |
| expiresAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| url | no | intersection | {"allOf":[{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":8192,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."},{"type":"string","pattern":"^(?!.*\\s)https:\\/\\/\\S+$"}]} |
| auth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| error | no | ["login_failed","expired","cancelled"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": {
    "source": "sdk-store",
    "status": "logged-in"
  },
  "expiresAt": 2,
  "instanceId": "HGs9wx2jTyqR-kUaZIdODT1iZV-rPCJx2xiNQvUecN3Jg-9LW9KfjeaNW",
  "loginId": "example",
  "requestId": "example",
  "state": "starting",
  "type": "cursor.auth.login",
  "url": "https://example.invalid/"
}
```

### cursor.auth.changed

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"cursor.auth.changed"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| instanceId | yes | [AccountId.json](schema/AccountId.json) |  |
| selectedInstanceId | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| auth | yes | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": {
    "source": "sdk-store",
    "status": "logged-out"
  },
  "instanceId": "7MFrHkLlsY6BBA1tZ2MBR6QkGQfmEI7Ev2eOLzaC4VvjWarPKvpPwJvQntrknljq6hlbdB7alkhO7M0OAcERhuCYUWJiyfpFah",
  "requestId": "example",
  "selectedInstanceId": "E1QcwjF84VbJZIeZc3ycym4OVefdDZGa7Xl5yHLMSaMwESoiXwpfvNiOi-VCDA",
  "type": "cursor.auth.changed"
}
```

### cursor.auth.error

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"cursor.auth.error"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| code | yes | ["unavailable","busy","not_found","forbidden","auth_failed"] |  |
| reason | no | ["service_unavailable","instance_unavailable","sdk_unavailable"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "code": "unavailable",
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "cursor.auth.error"
}
```

## CursorSdkAuth

[JSON Schema](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| status | yes | ["logged-in","logged-out"] |  |
| source | yes | ["environment","sdk-store","none"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "source": "none",
  "status": "logged-out"
}
```

## DelegationOutcome

[JSON Schema](schema/DelegationOutcome.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| outcome | yes | ["completed","failed","cancelled"] |  |
| result | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| truncated | yes | boolean |  |
| before | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "before": 5,
  "outcome": "cancelled",
  "result": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "truncated": true
}
```

## DelegationPolicy

[JSON Schema](schema/DelegationPolicy.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| maxConcurrent | no | integer | {"default":4,"minimum":1,"maximum":64} |
| maxChildren | no | integer | {"default":64,"minimum":1,"maximum":64} |
| maxDepth | no | integer | {"default":4,"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |
| durationMs | no | integer | {"default":3600000,"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":86400000} |
| tokens | no | integer | {"default":1000000,"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cost | no | number | {"default":100,"exclusiveMinimum":0} |
| coalesceMs | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":1000} |

```json
  "coalesceMs": 7,
  "maxChildren": 5
}
```

## DelegationRecord

[JSON Schema](schema/DelegationRecord.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| childId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| parentId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| parentAgentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| rootId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| request | yes | [DelegationRequest.json](schema/DelegationRequest.json) |  |
| depth | yes | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| phase | yes | ["created","running","cancelling","settled"] |  |
| generation | no | integer | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| outcome | no | [DelegationOutcome.json](schema/DelegationOutcome.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "childId": "example",
  "createdAt": 7,
  "depth": 2,
  "generation": 6,
  "parentAgentId": "example",
  "parentId": "example",
  "phase": "running",
  "request": {
    "estimatedLoad": 5,
    "installationId": "example",
    "options": {},
    "provider": "opencode",
    "requestId": "2s8EgU-rHs",
    "role": "example",
    "task": "example"
  },
  "requestId": "DFX5U7N-p",
  "rootId": "example"
}
```

## DelegationRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/DelegationRequest.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| task | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":16384} |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":1024} |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| options | no | [AgentLaunchOptions.json](schema/AgentLaunchOptions.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

```json
  "installationId": "example",

}
```

```json
}
```

| url | yes | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |

| type | yes | `"pluginResult"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":80} |

Example:

```json

  "requestId": "example",
  "response": {

    "installs": [],
```

## PortableHandoff

[JSON Schema](schema/PortableHandoff.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| sourceThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| origin | no | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"backend":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["provider"]} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | `"recent-complete-items"` |  |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |
| omittedItems | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| history | yes | object | {"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"items.page"},"tool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff"},"chunkTool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff_chunk","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff_chunk"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"before":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"type":"number","const":50}},"required":["type","threadId","before","limit"]} |
| limitations | yes | `"Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments."` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "excerpts": [],
  "history": {
    "before": 8,
    "limit": 50,
    "threadId": "example",
    "tool": "ace_read_handoff",
    "type": "items.page"
  },
  "limitations": "Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments.",
  "lossy": true,
  "omittedItems": 8,
  "policy": "recent-complete-items",
  "sourceThreadId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 8,
  "version": 1
}
```

## PreviewLaunch

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewLaunch.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Choose exactly one executable, or an attach URL without an executable or autoPort.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| name | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| command | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| runtimeExecutable | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| args | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096}} |
| cwd | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| env | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| port | no | [PreviewPort.json](schema/PreviewPort.json) |  |
| autoPort | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":4096,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |

Example:

```json

  "command": "node",
  "name": "example"
}
```

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "acpAgentId": "local:agent",
  "env": {},
  "homeDir": "/example/home",
  "homeStrategy": "default_cli",
  "id": "installed:default",
  "installationId": "installed",
  "instanceId": "installed:default",
  "label": "Local agent",
  "provider": "acp"
}
```

```json
  "before": [],

  ]
}
```

## ScreenCapabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `2` |  |
| platform | yes | ["macos","windows","linux-x11","linux-wayland"] |  |
| capture | yes | object | {"properties":{"windows":{"type":"boolean"},"displays":{"type":"boolean"},"changeDriven":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["windows","displays","changeDriven"]} |
| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |
| uiTree | yes | boolean |  |
| semanticActions | yes | array | {"maxItems":6,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["press","focus","setValue","scroll","expand","select"]}} |
| codecs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["jpeg","h264"]}} |
| permissions | yes | object | {"properties":{"screen":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]},"input":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]}},"required":["screen","input"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capture": {
    "changeDriven": true,
    "displays": true,
    "windows": true
  },
  "codecs": [
    "h264"
  ],
  "input": {
    "keyboard": false,
    "pointer": true,
    "scroll": false,
    "text": true
  },
  "permissions": {
    "input": "prompt",
    "screen": "granted"
  },
  "platform": "macos",
  "semanticActions": [],
  "uiTree": false,
  "version": 2
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| screen | yes | ["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"] |  |
| input | yes | ["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "input": "granted",
  "screen": "denied"
}
```

## ScreenRect

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenRect.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| x | yes | number |  |
| y | yes | number |  |
| w | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| h | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |

Example:

```json
{
  "h": 0,
  "w": 0,
  "x": 4,
  "y": 8
}
```

## ScreenServerMessage

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenServerMessage.json), input validation.

### screen.state

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"screen.state"` |  |
| state | yes | [ScreenState.json](schema/ScreenState.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "state": {
    "controller": "human",
    "error": "example",
    "indicator": true,
    "lifecycle": "stopping",
    "permissions": {
      "accessibility": true,
      "screenRecording": false
    },
    "sessionId": "waPWMQJg7DVAhE8YH542q6iTeBxcpIVPRSxGmdi1zEJWYbeVgetZ2z9gF",
    "target": {
      "bundleId": "example",
      "displayId": 10,
      "kind": "app"
    }
  },
  "type": "screen.state"
}
```

### screen.result

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"screen.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| ok | yes | boolean |  |
| data | no | any JSON value |  |
| error | no | string |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "data": null,
  "ok": false,
  "requestId": "Y-A5tapLkDEciTLyY3MTWLt7NcP0zF2PWlqjLcV7udmHA6ITMjs",
  "type": "screen.result"
}
```

## ScreenState

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenState.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| sessionId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| lifecycle | yes | ["starting","live","stopping","stopped","failed"] |  |
| controller | yes | ["agent","human","none"] |  |
| indicator | yes | boolean |  |
| target | yes | [ScreenTarget.json](schema/ScreenTarget.json) |  |
| permissions | yes | [ScreenPermissions.json](schema/ScreenPermissions.json) |  |
| error | no | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| capabilities | no | [ScreenCapabilities.json](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "controller": "agent",
  "indicator": false,
  "lifecycle": "failed",
  "permissions": {
    "accessibility": true,
    "screenRecording": false
  },
  "sessionId": "0G0AbVS-ZBQdG3ZjsrY7gK2S76qkkE",
  "target": {
    "bundleIds": [
      "example"
    ],
    "displayId": 6,
    "kind": "display"
  }
}
```

## ScreenTarget

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenTarget.json), input validation.

### app

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"app"` |  |
| bundleId | yes | [ScreenBundle.json](schema/ScreenBundle.json) |  |
| displayId | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

```json

  "bundleId": "example",
  "displayId": 2,
  "kind": "app"
}
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"window"` |  |
| bundleId | yes | [ScreenBundle.json](schema/ScreenBundle.json) |  |
| windowId | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

Example:

```json

  "bundleId": "example",
  "kind": "window",
  "windowId": 3
}
```

### display

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"display"` |  |
| displayId | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| bundleIds | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenBundle.json"}} |

Example:

```json

    "example"
  ],
  "displayId": 7,
  "kind": "display"
}
```

## ScreenTransportFrameHeader

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenTransportFrameHeader.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `2` |  |
| sessionId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| ts | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| width | yes | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":3840} |
| height | yes | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":2160} |
| scale | yes | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0} |
| captureGeneration | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| codec | yes | `"jpeg"` |  |
| bytes | yes | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8388608} |
| dirtyRects | no | array | {"maxItems":32,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenRect.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "bytes": 4,
  "codec": "jpeg",
  "dirtyRects": [],
  "height": 9,
  "scale": 5,
  "seq": 9,
  "sessionId": "qcAN2cvg6mnkK",
  "ts": 2,
  "version": 2,
  "width": 6
}
```

```json
  "states": []
}
```

## SearchFilters

[JSON Schema](schema/SearchFilters.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: after must be <= before when both timestamps are present.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| after | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| before | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| status | no | [SearchState.json](schema/SearchState.json) |  |
| agentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "before": 2
}
```

## SearchHit

[JSON Schema](schema/SearchHit.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| threadTitle | yes | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| itemId | no | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| agentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| status | yes | [SearchState.json](schema/SearchState.json) |  |
| statusSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| kind | yes | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| title | yes | [SearchSnippet.json](schema/SearchSnippet.json) |  |
| snippet | yes | [SearchSnippet.json](schema/SearchSnippet.json) |  |
| score | yes | number |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "createdAt": 1,
  "kind": "compaction",
  "provider": "acp",
  "score": 1,
  "snippet": {
    "highlights": [],
    "text": "example"
  },
  "status": "needs_you",
  "statusSeq": 6,
  "threadId": "example",
  "threadTitle": "example",
  "title": {
    "highlights": [],
    "text": "example"
  },
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```

| priceVersion | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| rows | yes | array | {"maxItems":1000,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageRow.json"}} |
| truncated | yes | boolean |  |
| burn | no | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageBurn.json"}} |

Example:

```json

  "cursor": 1,

  "timezone": "example",
  "truncated": false
```
```json
}
```

## UsageUpdated

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageUpdated.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: If usageScope is provider_session or model_session, counterKey is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| usageScope | no | ["agent","provider_session","model_session"] |  |
| counterKey | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| contextTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| contextSessionId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| reasoningTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cacheWriteTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cacheWrite1hTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1} |
| accountId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| billingMode | no | ["api","subscription","unknown"] |  |
| counterMode | no | ["cumulative","incremental"] |  |
| type | yes | `"usage.updated"` |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| inputTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| outputTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cachedInputTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| contextWindow | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| costUsd | no | number | {"minimum":0} |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "cacheWrite1hTokens": 2,
  "cachedInputTokens": 4,
  "contextSessionId": "example",
  "counterKey": "example",
  "counterMode": "incremental",
  "inputTokens": 7,
  "outputTokens": 3,
  "type": "usage.updated",
  "usageScope": "agent"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/WebPushSubscription.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| endpoint | yes | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |

```json
}
```
